### skills:root — 10 findings (0H/3M/7L), 44 files read

Verified statically and found CORRECT (no finding): package version 1.2.0 (src/medvision_bm/__version__.py) and every pyproject fact in repo-provenance (name, requires-python >=3.9, the 9 pinned deps, console script `mvbm`, package-data); commit 780e247d393bafba6ac71bb9707b0e0e4506d2e7, branch master, `v1.2.0-11-g780e247`, and the 8 dirty paths (exact `git status --porcelain` match); every count in `counts_at_snapshot` (21 eval__*.py, 24 launchers x 3 families, 20 AVAILABLE_MODELS keys, 22 dataset task dirs, 199 `*_base*.yaml`, 1253 YAMLs carrying a `task:` key); lmms_eval 0.3.0; medvision_ds 1.4.0 and its declared deps incl. the deliberate `huggingface_hub>=0.35.3,<2.0` floor (Data/src/pyproject.toml); the 25 requirements files and EVERY torch/torchvision/vllm/transformers/accelerate/huggingface_hub pin in the model-roster table (all 18 rows matched exactly); all 21 Docker tags; the whole token-budget paragraph (4096 local / 16000 API default, MiniMax pair 16384 x3 tasks, exactly five other local launchers at 16000, GPT launchers at 4096, and "no task YAML sets a budget" — 0 hits for max_new_tokens in tasks/*.yaml); `ENV_NAME="eval-<model>"`; the parallelism section (matches docs/Model-Parallelism-Summary.md and the prepare_model/device_map split in the six HF wrappers); MiniMax-M3 and GLM VRAM numbers (856/428/214 GB, 215 GB, 21 GB) and TP guidance; Claude's 28-grid rule (claude.py:85-123); `tasks_to_configs` semantics incl. the un-stripped `-CoT` trap; `resolve_plan_path` ceiling rule and `benchmark_plan_<type>_v<version>.json.gz` naming; the task-list filenames/shapes under tasks_list/{,OOD,experimental}; the `Data/` layout (.cache/huggingface, .downloaded_datasets.json, Datasets/, src/); `completed_tasks/completed_tasks_<task_tag>.json` and `Results/<task_tag>/`; response_cache prompt-hash keying and `MEDVISION_RESP_CACHE=0`; `--parsed_dirname`/`--resps_key`/`--limit` on the summarizers; `--tasks_json`/`--configs_csv` on download_datasets; the detection prompt (4 relative numbers, lower-left origin), the <10 px cluster + single-instance rules, 70/30 subject split, 121K/110K/5.5K/5.5K SFT recipe, `r = r_format + r_process*r_answer`, sequential A/D->T/L->detection, verl branch `medvision-rl`; paper software versions (medvision_bm 1.1.0 / medvision_ds 1.1.0 / verl 0.7.0, 4xH100, 4xH200); every entry in visualization-catalog.md (all 30+ script names, roster YAMLs, `--page_dir`, pypdf, C_GT_BOX #2ECC71 / C_PRED_BOX #F37020); all cross-file relative links in the unit resolve to existing files. NOT VERIFIED: (a) claims owned by the external `medvision_ds` loader script rather than this checkout — that `MedVision_PLANNER_VERSION` is *required* and that `MedVision_ACK_RELEASE` gates older pins (documented in README.md:140 but the enforcing loader script is not in Data/src/medvision_ds); (b) that `medvision_ds`'s `gdrive` dependency pins `setuptools~=59.6.0` and that `<81` is needed for pylidc (no such string anywhere in the checkout — external package metadata, no network); (c) the "~1 TB for the full collection" download budget (README documents the 298 GB QC-figure figure but no total); (d) `repo-routing-metadata.json`'s `taxonomy_sha256` and area/family assignments (skill-library infrastructure, no repo-side referent); (e) anything requiring a GPU/network — vLLM TP behaviour, actual perceived sizes from real HF processors, judge reproducibility, download semantics ("one config downloads the whole source dataset"). SEPARATE (not filed as a finding since it is a script defect, not a codebase inconsistency): scripts/check_medvision_env.py:308 is `print("secrets set …: " + ", ".join(...) or "none")` — `+` binds tighter than `or`, so the always-truthy concatenation means the "none" fallback can never print; the line emits a bare "secrets set (values never printed): " when no secret is set. ### skills:analysis — 5 findings (0H/3M/2L), 21 files read

Verification method and coverage: every .md, .py, .sh and .yaml file in skills/medvision/sub-skills/analysis/ was read (the four __pycache__ .pyc directories were skipped as build artifacts). All eleven `--help` blocks quoted in references/cli-reference.md were regenerated and compared: the two shell wrappers were byte-compared programmatically against the doc's ```text blocks (both exact matches), and the four bundled analyzers, three CDA scripts and four `medvision_bm.benchmark` detection modules were run with `--help` and compared line by line (all matched).
